the\n    ``report`` field carries structured ``CapabilityStatus`` entries while\n    the other five carry plain identifiers (capability names or eval ids /\n    CLI flags) so they can be diffed and serialised cheaply.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    report: tuple[CapabilityStatus, ...] = field(default_factory=tuple)\n    blocked_evals: tuple[str, ...] = field(default_factory=tuple)\n    skip_flags: tuple[str, ...] = field(default_factory=tuple)\n    hard_failures: tuple[str, ...] = field(default_factory=tuple)\n    soft_skips: tuple[str, ...] = field(default_factory=tuple)\n    soft_xfails: tuple[str, ...] = field(default_factory=tuple)\n\n    def to_json(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Return a JSON-serialisable mapping per DM-008.\n\n        Keys are emitted in the same order as the dataclass fields so the\n        ``eval doctor --json`` payload is stable across invocations. List\n        ordering inside each field is preserved as supplied by the caller\n        — downstream doctor snapshot tests (T01.13) may sort independently\n        if byte-level determinism is required.\n        \"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"report\": [status.to_dict() for status in self.report],\n            \"blocked_evals\": list(self.blocked_evals),\n            \"skip_flags\": list(self.skip_flags),\n            \"hard_failures\": list(self.hard_failures),\n            \"soft_skips\": list(self.soft_skips),\n            \"soft_xfails\": list(self.soft_xfails),\n        }\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# COMP-009 CapabilityGates (T01.11 / D-0010)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass _CapabilitySpec:\n    \"\"\"Static descriptor of one capability the harness checks.\n\n    Internal to this module — keeps the binary lookup target and the\n    classification metadata together so :class:`CapabilityGates` can\n    build ``Capability`` instances and ``CapabilityStatus`` rows from a\n    single source of truth without repeating the binary name.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    name: str\n    target: str\n    kind: Literal[\"binary\", \"mcp_server\"]\n    failure_mode: FailureMode\n    skip_flag: Optional[str]\n    description: str\n\n\n# Roster ordering matches design-spec §11 (HARD binaries first, then\n# SOFT-SKIP MCP servers). ``CapabilityGates`` emits ``report[]`` rows in\n# this order, which is what gives ``eval doctor`` (T01.13) its stable\n# green-checklist layout.\n_DEFAULT_CAPABILITY_SPECS: tuple[_CapabilitySpec, ...] = (\n    _CapabilitySpec(\n        \"binary.claude\", \"claude\", \"binary\", \"hard\", None,\n        \"Claude CLI on PATH\",\n    ),\n    _CapabilitySpec(\n        \"binary.make\", \"make\", \"binary\", \"hard\", None,\n        \"GNU make on PATH\",\n    ),\n    _CapabilitySpec(\n        \"binary.jq\", \"jq\", \"binary\", \"hard\", None,\n        \"jq JSON processor on PATH\",\n    ),\n    _CapabilitySpec(\n        \"binary.git\", \"git\", \"binary\", \"hard\", None,\n        \"git VCS on PATH\",\n    ),\n    _CapabilitySpec(\n        \"mcp_server.auggie\", \"auggie\", \"mcp_server\", \"skip\", \"--no-mcp\",\n        \"Auggie MCP server reachable\",\n    ),\n    _CapabilitySpec(\n        \"mcp_server.auggie-mcp\", \"auggie-mcp\", \"mcp_server\", \"skip\", \"--no-mcp\",\n        \"auggie-mcp MCP server reachable\",\n    ),\n    _CapabilitySpec(\n        \"mcp_server.airis-mcp-gateway\", \"airis-mcp-gateway\",\n        \"mcp_server\", \"skip\", \"--no-mcp\",\n        \"AIRIS MCP gateway reachable\",\n    ),\n)\n\n\n# Type alias for the (passed, detail) tuple returned by per-target probes.\nProbeResult = tuple[bool, str]\n\n\nclass CapabilityGates:\n    \"\"\"COMP-009: evaluate every capability and emit a ``CapabilityReport``.\n\n    The gate is constructed once per ``eval doctor`` / ``eval run``\n    invocation. Pass the active CLI ``skip_flags`` (e.g. ``{\"--no-mcp\"}``)\n    so SOFT-SKIP capabilities whose descriptor names a matching flag are\n    forced to skipped status even when their underlying probe would pass.\n\n    The class is purposely small: ``check_all`` drives the static\n    :data:`_DEFAULT_CAPABILITY_SPECS` roster through :meth:`which_or_skip`\n    or :meth:`mcp_server_reachable` and assembles the report. Tests\n    inject a custom spec tuple via the ``capabilities`` keyword to mock\n    PATH state without monkeypatching ``shutil``.\n\n    OQ-5 (roadmap Open Questions): the precise ``mcp_server_reachable``\n    contract (PATH presence vs handshake vs SSE probe) must be resolved\n    before COMP-009 close at M2. The M1 implementation here uses PATH\n    presence as the reachability signal — see :meth:`mcp_server_reachable`\n    for the deferral note and the override hook.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        skip_flags: Optional[Iterable[str]] = None,\n        capabilities: Optional[Iterable[_CapabilitySpec]] = None,\n        mcp_probe: Optional[Callable[[str], ProbeResult]] = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._skip_flags: frozenset[str] = frozenset(skip_flags or ())\n        self._specs: tuple[_CapabilitySpec, ...] = tuple(\n            capabilities if capabilities is not None else _DEFAULT_CAPABILITY_SPECS\n        )\n        # Test hook so ``mcp_server_reachable`` can be redirected without\n        # monkeypatching the module. Production callers leave this ``None``\n        # and the default PATH-presence probe (OQ-5 M1 stub) is used.\n        self._mcp_probe = mcp_probe\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # public API\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    @property\n    def skip_flags(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:\n        \"\"\"Active CLI skip flags, sorted for deterministic report output.\"\"\"\n        return tuple(sorted(self._skip_flags))\n\n    def capabilities(self) -> tuple[Capability, ...]:\n        \"\"\"Materialise the static spec table as ``Capability`` instances.\n\n        Each ``Capability.check`` is a closure that defers to\n        :meth:`which_or_skip` / :meth:`mcp_server_reachable` so the\n        descriptors stay in sync with the gate's own probing — there is no\n        second copy of the lookup logic in the closures.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        return tuple(self._make_capability(spec) for spec in self._specs)\n\n    def which_or_skip(self, name: str) -> ProbeResult:\n        \"\"\"Probe whether binary ``name`` is on ``PATH``.\n\n        Returns ``(True, \"<resolved-path>\")`` when ``shutil.which`` finds\n        the binary, ``(False, \"not found on PATH\")`` otherwise. The\n        ``detail`` half is carried verbatim onto the\n        :class:`CapabilityStatus` row so ``eval doctor`` can print the\n        resolved path on success or the failure reason on miss.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        path = shutil.which(name)\n        if path is None:\n            return False, \"not found on PATH\"\n        return True, path\n\n    def mcp_server_reachable(self, name: str) -> ProbeResult:\n        \"\"\"Probe whether MCP server ``name`` is reachable.\n\n        OQ-5 deferral: the canonical reachability semantics (PATH\n        presence vs full stdio handshake vs SSE health probe) are open\n        and must resolve before COMP-009 closes at M2. Until then, this\n        implementation treats \"binary on PATH\" as the reachability\n        signal because every MCP server in the default roster\n        (``auggie``, ``auggie-mcp``, ``airis-mcp-gateway``) ships as an\n        executable.\n\n        Tests inject a custom probe via the ``mcp_probe`` constructor\n        argument; the M2 follow-up will land a real handshake probe\n        under the same signature.\n        \"\"\"\n\n        if self._mcp_probe is not None:\n            return self._mcp_probe(name)\n        path = shutil.which(name)\n        if path is None:\n            return False, \"MCP server binary not on PATH\"\n        return True, path\n\n    def check_all(self) -> CapabilityReport:\n        \"\"\"Run every spec through its probe and assemble the report.\n\n        Returns:\n            A :class:`CapabilityReport` whose ``report`` tuple has one\n            :class:`CapabilityStatus` row per spec in declaration order.\n            ``hard_failures`` / ``soft_skips`` / ``soft_xfails`` carry\n            the capability names that failed (or were skip-flagged) per\n            their declared ``failure_mode``. ``blocked_evals`` is empty\n            here — the SuiteLoader (T01.07) is the layer that knows\n            which evals depend on which capability and populates that\n            list at run time.\n\n            Calling :meth:`check_all` twice on the same gate instance\n            produces equal reports (idempotence is required by the\n            T01.11 acceptance criteria; verified by\n            ``test_check_all_is_idempotent``).\n        \"\"\"\n\n        rows: list[CapabilityStatus] = []\n        hard_failures: list[str] = []\n        soft_skips: list[str] = []\n        soft_xfails: list[str] = []\n\n        for spec in self._specs:\n            passed, detail = self._probe(spec)\n            skipped_by_flag = False\n            if (\n                spec.skip_flag is not None\n                and spec.skip_flag in self._skip_flags\n            ):\n                # SOFT-SKIP override: the user asked us to ignore this\n                # capability tier regardless of probe result. The probe\n                # detail is preserved so doctor output can still show\n                # why the capability would have passed/failed without\n                # the flag.\n                skipped_by_flag = True\n                passed = False\n\n            rows.append(\n                CapabilityStatus(\n                    name=spec.name,\n                    passed=passed,\n                    failure_mode=spec.failure_mode,\n                    description=spec.description,\n                    detail=detail,\n                    skipped_by_flag=skipped_by_flag,\n                )\n            )\n\n            if not passed:\n                if spec.failure_mode == \"hard\":\n                    hard_failures.append(spec.name)\n                elif spec.failure_mode == \"skip\":\n                    soft_skips.append(spec.name)\n                else:  # \"xfail\"\n                    soft_xfails.append(spec.name)\n\n        return CapabilityReport(\n            report=tuple(rows),\n            blocked_evals=(),\n            skip_flags=self.skip_flags,\n            hard_failures=tuple(hard_failures),\n            soft_skips=tuple(soft_skips),\n            soft_xfails=tuple(soft_xfails),\n        )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # internals\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _probe(self, spec: _CapabilitySpec) -> ProbeResult:\n        if spec.kind == \"binary\":\n            return self.which_or_skip(spec.target)\n        if spec.kind == \"mcp_server\":\n            return self.mcp_server_reachable(spec.target)\n        # Defensive — the Literal annotation prevents this at type-check\n        # time, but the runtime guard keeps a typo in a custom spec\n        # tuple from silently producing an always-passing row.\n        raise ValueError(f\"unknown capability kind: {spec.kind!r}\")\n\n    def _make_capability(self, spec: _CapabilitySpec) -> Capability:\n        if spec.kind == \"binary\":\n            def _check(target: str = spec.target) -> bool:\n                return self.which_or_skip(target)[0]\n        else:\n            def _check(target: str = spec.target) -> bool:\n                return self.mcp_server_reachable(target)[0]\n        return Capability(\n            name=spec.name,\n            check=_check,\n            failure_mode=spec.failure_mode,\n            skip_flag=spec.skip_flag,\n            description=spec.description,\n        )\n","numLines":410,"startLine":1,"totalLines":410}}}

status.failure_mode == \"hard\":\n            hard_failures.append(status.name)\n        elif status.failure_mode == \"skip\":\n            soft_skips.append(status.name)\n        else:  # \"xfail\"\n            soft_xfails.append(status.name)\n    return replace(\n        base,\n        report=rows,\n        hard_failures=tuple(hard_failures),\n        soft_skips=tuple(soft_skips),\n        soft_xfails=tuple(soft_xfails),\n    )\n\n\ndef build_doctor_report(\n    skip_flags: Optional[Iterable[str]] = None,\n    *,\n    gates: Optional[CapabilityGates] = None,\n    claude_version_probe: Optional[Callable[[], Optional[str]]] = None,\n    claude_home: Optional[Path] = None,\n    pty_dir: Optional[Path] = None,\n) -> CapabilityReport:\n    \"\"\"Aggregate :class:`CapabilityGates` + doctor-specific checks.\n\n    Args:\n        skip_flags: Active CLI skip flags (e.g. ``{\"--no-mcp\"}``).\n            Forwarded to :class:`CapabilityGates` when ``gates`` is not\n            supplied.\n        gates: Optional pre-built :class:`CapabilityGates` (test hook).\n        claude_version_probe: Override for ``claude --version`` invocation.\n        claude_home: Override for ``~/.claude/`` path.\n        pty_dir: Override for the ptytest vendored directory.\n\n    Returns:\n        A :class:`CapabilityReport` whose ``report`` tuple contains the\n        seven default capability rows followed by the three doctor rows\n        in this fixed order: ``claude.min_version``,\n        ``filesystem.claude_home``, ``vendored.ptytest``.\n    \"\"\"\n    if gates is None:\n        gates = CapabilityGates(skip_flags=skip_flags or ())\n    base = gates.check_all()\n    extras = (\n        _check_claude_version(probe=claude_version_probe),\n        _check_claude_home(home=claude_home),\n        _check_ptytest_vendored(pty_dir=pty_dir),\n    )\n    return _extend_report(base, extras)\n\n\ndef _row_marker(row: CapabilityStatus) -> tuple[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Return ``(glyph, tag)`` for the human checklist line.\"\"\"\n    if row.passed:\n        return \"[ok]\", \"\"\n    if row.skipped_by_flag:\n        return \"[--]\", \" (skipped by flag)\"\n    if row.failure_mode == \"hard\":\n        return \"[XX]\", \" (HARD)\"\n    if row.failure_mode == \"skip\":\n        return \"[--]\", \" (SOFT-SKIP)\"\n    return \"[??]\", \" (xfail)\"\n\n\ndef render_checklist(report: CapabilityReport) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Render the doctor green-checklist text (FR-CLI4 human output).\"\"\"\n    lines = [\"superclaude eval doctor:\"]\n    for row in report.report:\n        marker, tag = _row_marker(row)\n        detail = f\" -- {row.detail}\" if row.detail else \"\"\n        lines.append(f\"  {marker} {row.description}{tag}{detail}\")\n    if report.skip_flags:\n        lines.append(f\"skip flags: {', '.join(report.skip_flags)}\")\n    if report.hard_failures:\n        lines.append(f\"HARD failures: {', '.join(report.hard_failures)}\")\n    elif report.soft_skips:\n        lines.append(f\"soft skips: {', '.join(report.soft_skips)}\")\n    else:\n        lines.append(\"all HARD capabilities satisfied\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\ndef render_hard_failure_artifact(report: CapabilityReport) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Render the stderr HARD-failure artifact per FR-CLI4 AC.\"\"\"\n    by_name = {row.name: row for row in report.report}\n    lines = [\"eval doctor: HARD failures\"]\n    for name in report.hard_failures:\n        row = by_name.get(name)\n        if row is None:\n            lines.append(f\"  - {name}\")\n            continue\n        lines.append(f\"  - {name} ({row.description}): {row.detail}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\ndef doctor_payload(report: CapabilityReport, *, check_coverage: bool) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Build the ``--json`` payload.\n\n    Extends :meth:`CapabilityReport.to_json` with a ``coverage_gate``\n    marker so callers can detect whether ``--check-coverage`` was passed.\n    The base ``CapabilityReport`` keys are preserved verbatim so the\n    payload remains compatible with the D-0010 contract.\n    \"\"\"\n    payload = report.to_json()\n    payload[\"coverage_gate\"] = {\n        \"requested\": bool(check_coverage),\n        \"status\": \"deferred\",\n        \"milestone\": \"M4\",\n        \"task\": \"T04.14\",\n    }\n    return payload\n\n\nHARD_FAIL_EXIT_CODE = 2\n\n\n@click.group(\"eval\")\ndef eval_group() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Run and inspect the cliEval real-eval harness.\"\"\"\n\n\n@eval_group.command(\"doctor\")\n@click.option(\n    \"--json\",\n    \"as_json\",\n    is_flag=True,\n    help=\"Emit a deterministic JSON payload extending \"--check-coverage\",\n    is_flag=True,\n    help=(\n        \"Wire the FR-G5 coverage gate marker into the payload \"\n        \"(full gate lands in M4 T04.14).\"\n    ),\n)\ndef doctor(as_json: bool, no_mcp: bool, check_coverage: bool) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Verify host preconditions for ``superclaude eval run``.\n\n    Exit codes:\n\n    * ``0`` — every HARD capability satisfied.\n    * ``2`` sort_keys=True))\n    else:\n        click.echo(render_checklist(report))\n        if check_coverage:\n            click.echo(\"coverage gate: deferred to M4 T04.14\")\n\n    if only when CapabilityReport.skip_flags is non-empty\nall HARD capabilities satisfied | HARD failures: <names>\nsoft skips: <names>        # only when there are no HARD failures\n```\n\nRow markers are emitted by `_row_marker(row)` and never use shell colour\ncodes so the output is safe to capture in CI logs.\n\n## JSON payload schema\n\nThe payload is `CapabilityReport.to_json()` plus a single extension key:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"report\":         [ {CapabilityStatus rows…} ],\n  \"blocked_evals\":  [],\n  \"skip_flags\":     [],\n  \"hard_failures\":  [],\n  \"soft_skips\":     [],\n  \"soft_xfails\":    [],\n  \"coverage_gate\": {\n    \"requested\":  bool,\n    \"status\":     \"deferred\",\n    \"milestone\":  \"M4\",\n    \"task\":       \"T04.14\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n`json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True)` guarantees byte-level\ndeterminism across invocations on a stable host (verified by\n`test_cli_doctor_json_is_deterministic_across_invocations`).\n\nThe `report[]` list ordering is fixed: the seven default capability rows\n(from `_DEFAULT_CAPABILITY_SPECS`) in declaration order, followed by the\nthree doctor-specific rows in this fixed order:\n\n1. `claude.min_version`     — HARD\n2. `filesystem.claude_home` — HARD\n3. `vendored.ptytest`       — SOFT-SKIP\n\n## Supplementary capability rows (T01.13-specific)\n\n`CapabilityGates` covers the binary / MCP roster per `D-0010`. The\ndoctor command appends three rows via `build_doctor_report()`:\n\n| Row name | Failure mode | Probe | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `claude.min_version` | hard | `claude --version` parsed for `>=0.5.0` | Probe is injectable via `_default_claude_version_probe`; fails closed when binary missing, output unparseable, or below floor. |\n| `filesystem.claude_home` | hard | `Path.home() / \".claude\"` is_dir | Override via `claude_home=` for tests. |\n| `vendored.ptytest` | skip | `<cli/eval>/pty/__init__.py` is_file | SOFT-SKIP until M2 vendoring task; lets M1 doctor exit 0 on a clean dev machine. |\n\n## HARD-failure artifact\n\nWhen `report.hard_failures` is non-empty, `render_hard_failure_artifact`\nemits the following stderr block (the \"artifact\" mandated by FR-CLI4\nAC):\n\n```\neval doctor: HARD failures\n  - <name> (<description>): <detail>\n  - <name> (<description>): <detail>\n```\n\nThe artifact is emitted regardless of `--json` / human mode so CI logs\nalways capture a stable per-failure diagnostic line.\n\n## Test injection seams\n\n| Seam | How tests use it |\n|---|---|\n| `commands._default_claude_version_probe` | `monkeypatch.setattr` to return a literal version banner or `None`. |\n| `shutil.which` | `monkeypatch.setattr` to fake PATH state without touching the host. |\n| `Path.home` | `monkeypatch.setattr` to redirect `~/.claude/` resolution to a tmp dir. |\n| `build_doctor_report(gates=…, claude_version_probe=…, claude_home=…, pty_dir=…)` | Direct kwargs for unit tests that need full control of every probe. |\n\n## Wiring\n\n* `eval_group` added to `superclaude.cli.main:main` via\n  `main.add_command(eval_group, name=\"eval\")`.\n* `commands.eval_group` and `commands.doctor` use Click 8.3-compatible\n  decorators (no removed `mix_stderr` kwarg).\n\n## Acceptance criteria → implementation map\n\n| AC bullet (T01.13) | Implementation site |\n|---|---|\n| `superclaude eval doctor` exits 0 on a clean dev machine. | `doctor` Click handler; covered by `test_cli_doctor_exits_zero_on_clean_host`. |\n| `--json` emits a deterministic JSON payload matching the `CapabilityReport` contract. | `doctor_payload` + `json.dumps(..., sort_keys=True)`; covered by `test_cli_doctor_json_payload_matches_report_contract` + `test_cli_doctor_json_is_deterministic_across_invocations`. |\n| Doctor fails closed (exit 2) when any HARD capability is missing; emits a HARD-failure artifact. | `sys.exit(HARD_FAIL_EXIT_CODE)` + `render_hard_failure_artifact`; covered by `test_cli_doctor_exits_two_when_hard_capability_missing` + `test_cli_doctor_json_exit_two_includes_hard_failures_in_payload`. |\n| `artifacts/D-0011/spec.md` documents the green-checklist format and JSON schema. | This file. |\n\n## Out of scope for T01.13\n\n- Full FR-G5 coverage gate evaluation — M4 T04.14 (the `--check-coverage` flag is wired now but only stamps a deferral marker).\n- Real ptytest vendoring under `cli/eval/pty/` — M2 follow-up.\n- Real MCP handshake probe (OQ-5) — M2 follow-up, override hook is\n  `CapabilityGates(mcp_probe=…)`.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":193,"cache_read_input_tokens":148717,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":193,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":60,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available","speed":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b040bb69-6d1c-4670-a09a-b2696d794966","uuid":"3f1573a4-47f2-4fed-bc65-741e0e49bc51"}
